                                                          M21 (drop 0.43): one attention head on the 2x2 mesh of 16x8 cores -- T = 64 tokens, model dim 32, head dim 32,
                                                          INT8 -- compiled by compiler/neo_xfmr.py's attention_plan: the projections (three of the twelve output tiles of Q, K
                                                          and V on each tile, in two halves of 32 tokens -- a descriptor drains at most ACC_ROWS - 1 rows --, every output tile
                                                          drained into two copies by a second GO on the same weights: Q padded into Q's activation layout, K transposed as S's
                                                          weight entries, V as P V's weight entries), the scores of query block t (16 queries) per key tile with the softmax's
                                                          max round and exp round on the vector unit (RS0 / RS1 across the eight key tiles; P drained padded as P V's
                                                          activations), P V with V's entries as the weights and the 1 / sum (reciprocal table and a Newton step) on the drain.
                                                          Every phase runs on one pair of tiles at a time, fetching from the other pair's banks (neo_xfmr's PAIRS: the NIC's
                                                          serve / send deadlock, stream B2's fix). The output rows equal the compiler's mesh model bit for bit and the model
                                                          equals the independent integer chain (attention_ref); no cause is raised; the error against the float attention and
                                                          the cycles per phase against the perf model are reported.

170868.52ns INFO     cocotb.regression                  running m22_self_serve (22/24)
                                                          M22 (drop 0.43, stream B2): the placement the chip-top suite avoided -- the tile that holds the layer's data computes
                                                          a share as well, fetching activations and weights from its own bank while it serves the other tiles' fetches. Before the
                                                          fix the other tiles' requests waited in that tile's receive stage in front of its own responses, which came back through
                                                          its router's local port, and every requester timed out (B1's finding). tile_nic now keeps a tile's fetch from its own
                                                          bank out of the mesh (the fetch goes to the server directly, the beats come back through the loopback register) and
                                                          holds a request from the link outside the receive stage while that serve is in progress or pending. Checked: the layer
                                                          bit-exact on all four tiles with every cause clean, and no flit from the data tile ever on its router's local output
                                                          (watched every cycle through the observation port).

162332.02ns INFO     cocotb.regression                  running m22_self_serve (22/24)
                                                          M22 (drop 0.43, stream B2): the placement the chip-top suite avoided -- the tile that holds the layer's data computes
                                                          a share as well, fetching activations and weights from its own bank while it serves the other tiles' fetches. Before the
                                                          fix the other tiles' requests waited in that tile's receive stage in front of its own responses, which came back through
                                                          its router's local port, and every requester timed out (B1's finding). tile_nic now keeps a tile's fetch from its own
                                                          bank out of the mesh (the fetch goes to the server directly, the beats come back through the loopback register) and
                                                          holds a request from the link outside the receive stage while that serve is in progress or pending. Checked: the layer
                                                          bit-exact on all four tiles with every cause clean, and no flit from the data tile ever on its router's local output
                                                          (watched every cycle through the observation port).

                                                          over two tiles (the contributor starts mid channel tile, at a nonzero cfg_ky0 / cfg_kx0). The owner's drained rows are
                                                          bit-exact against the dense convolution on the pruned weights and no cause is raised on any tile.

170868.52ns INFO     cocotb.regression                  running m22_self_serve (22/24)
                                                          M22 (drop 0.43, stream B2): the placement the chip-top suite avoided -- the tile that holds the layer's data computes
                                                          a share as well, fetching activations and weights from its own bank while it serves the other tiles' fetches. Before the
                                                          fix the other tiles' requests waited in that tile's receive stage in front of its own responses, which came back through
                                                          its router's local port, and every requester timed out (B1's finding). tile_nic now keeps a tile's fetch from its own
                                                          bank out of the mesh (the fetch goes to the server directly, the beats come back through the loopback register) and
                                                          holds a request from the link outside the receive stage while that serve is in progress or pending. Checked: the layer
                                                          bit-exact on all four tiles with every cause clean, and no flit from the data tile ever on its router's local output
                                                          (watched every cycle through the observation port).
